Use 201 for POST endpoints that exclusively create new resources.

schemas/constructs/v1beta3/component/api.yml	Schema "ComponentDefinition" mixes JSON-tag casing conventions across its properties - camel: [createdAt, deletedAt, displayName, instanceDetails, isAnnotation, isNamespaced, modelId, modelReference, schemaVersion, updatedAt]; lowercase: [capabilities, component, configuration, description, format, genealogy, id, metadata, model, published, status, styles, version]; screaming: [configurationUISchema]. Partial casing migrations are forbidden under the canonical identifier-naming contract. If the wire format must change, introduce a new API version and migrate every property consistently. See docs/casing-rules.md.
